					<div id="content" class="page-content ve-init-mw-desktopArticleTarget-targetContainer">
																		<div id="mw-content-text" class="mw-body-content"><!-- Google Tag Manager (noscript) -->
<noscript><iframe src="https://www.googletagmanager.com/ns.html?id=GTM-N6XD44P" height="0" width="0" style="display:none;visibility:hidden"></iframe></noscript>
<!-- End Google Tag Manager (noscript) -->
<div class="mw-content-ltr mw-parser-output" lang="en" dir="ltr"><figure class="thumb  show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 180px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/kumo-desu-ga-nani-ka/images/f/f7/Magic.png/revision/latest?cb=20190217020520" class="mw-file-description image" style="display: inline-block; position: relative;"><img alt="Magic" src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/kumo-desu-ga-nani-ka/images/f/f7/Magic.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20190217020520" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="180" height="141" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Magic.png" data-image-key="Magic.png" data-relevant="1"><div style="position: absolute; bottom: 0px; right: 0px; width: 100%; height: 100%;"><div class="gallery-icon-container view-image"><div class="icon-container view-image" aria-label="view image"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" viewBox="0 0 18 18" width="1em" height="1em"><defs><path id="IconImagesSmall__a" d="M11 15h-.586L3 7.586V7h8v8zm-8 0v-4.586L7.586 15H3zm9-10a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v10a1 1 0 0 1-1 1H2a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V6a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h10zm4-4a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v10a1 1 0 1 1-2 0V3H6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2h10z"></path></defs><use xlink:href="#IconImagesSmall__a" fill-rule="evenodd"></use></svg></div></div></div></a> 	 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			 		 		 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p><b>Magic</b> (魔法, <i>Mahō</i>) is a supernatural phenomenon in which the user exchanges MP in order to perform system-assisted <a href="/wiki/Magecraft" class="mw-redirect" title="Magecraft">magecraft</a>. In order to use magic the user must have <a href="/wiki/System/Skills" title="System/Skills">Skills</a> such as Magic Power Operation and Magic Power Perception.<sup id="cite_ref-LN1S5_1-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN1S5-1" aria-label="Reference 1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Additionally, the user must be able to construct the spell circuit, either by possessing the appropriate skill at a high enough level,<sup id="cite_ref-LN3C10_2-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN3C10-2" aria-label="Reference 2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> or by using a magic-imbued staff.<sup id="cite_ref-LN2S2_3-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2S2-3" aria-label="Reference 3"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div id="toc" class="toc toc--initialized" role="navigation" aria-labelledby="mw-toc-heading"><input type="checkbox" role="button" id="toctogglecheckbox" class="toctogglecheckbox" style="display:none" aria-label="show"><div class="toctitle" lang="en" dir="ltr"><h2 id="mw-toc-heading"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use href="#wds-icons-bullet-list-tiny"></use></svg>Contents</h2><span class="toctogglespan"><label class="toctogglelabel" for="toctogglecheckbox" aria-label="show"></label></span></div>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Magical_Ability">Magical Ability</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D1&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p>There are several stats that are a part of Magical Ability within the System. Many of these can be appraised, but most cannot. The ones that are visible during regular appraisals are <b>MP</b>, <b>Magic</b>, and <b>Resistance</b>. When Kumoko is granted <a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Other_Skills/Wisdom" title="System/Skills/Other Skills/Wisdom">Wisdom</a>, several other stats related to Magical Ability are revealed to her.<sup id="cite_ref-LN2C8_4-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2C8-4" aria-label="Reference 4"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> These are skills related to <b>Runes</b> (術式, <i>Jutsushiki</i>), which are the basis of magecraft. 
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Heresy">Heresy</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D2&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Heretic_Magic">Heretic Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D3&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>『Magic that directly attacks the soul.』<sup id="cite_ref-LN1C11_5-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN1C11-5" aria-label="Reference 5"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Intended as a tool to confront gods.<sup id="cite_ref-WN299_6-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WN299-6" aria-label="Reference 6"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>LV1: Discomfort. <i>Plants discomfort directly in the target's soul.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN1C11_5-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN1C11-5" aria-label="Reference 5"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV2: Phantom Pain. <i>Plants phantom pain directly in the target's soul,<sup id="cite_ref-LN1C11_5-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN1C11-5" aria-label="Reference 5"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> ignoring Pain Mitigation.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN3C11_7-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN3C11-7" aria-label="Reference 7"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV3: Phantom Insanity. <i>Plants insanity directly in the target's soul.</i><sup id="cite_ref-WN63_8-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WN63-8" aria-label="Reference 8"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV5: Hypnosis. <i>Forces the target to temporarily obey the user. Requires a complicated magic construction, and the MP cost increases with the target's resistance.</i><sup id="cite_ref-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-9" aria-label="Reference 9"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>9<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV6: Phantasm. <i>Induces hallucinations in the target.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN3C4_10-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN3C4-10" aria-label="Reference 10"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV10: Rend Soul. <i>Destroys the opponent's soul.</i><sup id="cite_ref-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-11" aria-label="Reference 11"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Unknown Charm magic. <i>Makes the target develop postive feelings towards the user.</i></li>
<li>Unknown Fear magic. <i>Plants fear directly in the target's soul.</i></li>
<li>Unknown Confusion magic. <i>Plants confusion directly in the target's soul.</i></li>
<li>Unknown Stupefication magic. <i>Reduces Target Intelligence/Comprehesive thinking.</i></li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Light">Light</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D4&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Light_Magic">Light Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D5&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>Light Barrier. <i>Produces a thin shield of light suitable for defending against low-powered attacks.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN2J2_12-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2J2-12" aria-label="Reference 12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Light Beam. <i>Low-powered magic that pierces.</i><sup id="cite_ref-13" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-13" aria-label="Reference 13"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV10: Light Field. <i>Bathes a broad area with light, dealing damage.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN2J2_12-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2J2-12" aria-label="Reference 12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Holy_Light_Magic">Holy Light Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D6&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>Holy Light Sphere. <i>Creates a small ball of light that bursts over the target.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN2J2_12-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2J2-12" aria-label="Reference 12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Mirror Shield. <i>Conjures a thin pane of light capable of reflecting any attack.</i><sup id="cite_ref-14" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-14" aria-label="Reference 14"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV7: Holy Light Beam. <i>Powerful attack that deploys in a straight line.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN2J2_12-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2J2-12" aria-label="Reference 12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Dark">Dark</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D7&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Shadow_Magic">Shadow Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D8&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>『Lower-ranking Dark Magic that manipulates shadows.』<sup id="cite_ref-LN1C11_5-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN1C11-5" aria-label="Reference 5"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ol><li>Dark Shadow. <i>Makes a shadow darker.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN1C11_5-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN1C11-5" aria-label="Reference 5"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Large Shadow. <i>Makes a shadow larger.</i><sup id="cite_ref-WN63_8-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WN63-8" aria-label="Reference 8"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Shadow Surface. <i>Shadows can be generated in the light.</i><sup id="cite_ref-15" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-15" aria-label="Reference 15"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Shadow Change. <i>Changes the shape of a shadow.</i><sup id="cite_ref-WN114_16-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WN114-16" aria-label="Reference 16"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Solid Shadow. <i>Solidifies a shadow.</i><sup id="cite_ref-WN114_16-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WN114-16" aria-label="Reference 16"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Shadow Manipulation. <i>Moves a shadow.</i><sup id="cite_ref-WN114_16-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WN114-16" aria-label="Reference 16"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Shadow Sink. <i>Sinks something into a shadow. The possible size of the target is limited by the size of the shadow. There is no air within the shadow, although it is easy to escape from.</i><sup id="cite_ref-17" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-17" aria-label="Reference 17"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ol>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Dark_Magic">Dark Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D9&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>LV1: Dark Bullet. <i>Fires a black ball.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN3C11_7-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN3C11-7" aria-label="Reference 7"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-LN4C3_18-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN4C3-18" aria-label="Reference 18"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Dark World. <i>Darkness gushes out, dealing moderate damage to anything that touches it.</i><sup id="cite_ref-19" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-19" aria-label="Reference 19"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Black_Magic">Black Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D10&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>Black Bullet. <i>Fires a jet-black ball which explodes with the Shock attribute upon collision.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN3C9_20-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN3C9-20" aria-label="Reference 20"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV3: Black Spear. <i>Spear version of Black Bullet. Deals piercing-attribute damage.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN3C9_20-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN3C9-20" aria-label="Reference 20"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV4: Black Blade. <i>Creates a jet-black blade that slashes at the target.</i><sup id="cite_ref-21" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-21" aria-label="Reference 21"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Black World. <i>Engulfs a 100m diameter in darkness. Slightly inferior to Hell Gate in terms of damage.</i><sup id="cite_ref-22" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-22" aria-label="Reference 22"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Abyss_Magic">Abyss Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D11&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>『The highest form of Dark Magic. Manipulates the darkness of the abyss』<sup id="cite_ref-LN2C2_23-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2C2-23" aria-label="Reference 23"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Killing with this magic produces no EXP.<sup id="cite_ref-24" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-24" aria-label="Reference 24"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Unlike Heretic Magic, which destroys the soul, Abyss Magic decomposes the soul and contributes its energy to the system, preventing the target from ever reincarnating. All Abyss Magic spells have a slow invocation time because their purpose is to pass judgement.<sup id="cite_ref-WN299_6-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WN299-6" aria-label="Reference 6"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ol><li>Hell Gate. <i>The beginning gate. An intense darkness seeps up from the ground, swallowing everything in a 100m radius and converging before seeping back into the ground, sinking it 5m lower.</i><sup id="cite_ref-25" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-25" aria-label="Reference 25"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Hell of Unbelievers. <i>Hell for the ignorant and unbelievers.</i></li>
<li>Hell of Lust. <i>Hell for those stained with lust.</i></li>
<li>Hell of Gluttony. <i>Hell for those who glorify gluttony.</i></li>
<li>Hell of Greed. <i>Hell for those who devote themselves to greed.</i></li>
<li>Hell of Wrath. <i>Hell for those who are ruled by wrath.</i></li>
<li>Hell of Heresy. <i>Hell for those who idolize heresy.</i></li>
<li>Hell of Violence. <i>Hell for those who exert violence.</i></li>
<li>Hell of Fraud. <i>Hell for those who whisper fraud.</i></li>
<li>Hell of Treachery. <i>Hell for those who stir up treachery. Countless jet-black inverted crosses rain down, erasing the target upon contact.</i><sup id="cite_ref-26" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-26" aria-label="Reference 26"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ol>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Poison">Poison</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D12&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Poison_Magic">Poison Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D13&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>『Magic that manipulates poison.』<sup id="cite_ref-LN1C11_5-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN1C11-5" aria-label="Reference 5"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>LV1: Poison Touch. <i>Adds a poison-attribute attack to a target upon contact.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN1C11_5-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN1C11-5" aria-label="Reference 5"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV2: Poison Shot. <i>Shoots a poison-attribute shot.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN2C8_4-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2C8-4" aria-label="Reference 4"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV3: Poison Resist. <i>Temporarily increases the target's poison resistance.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN2C8_4-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2C8-4" aria-label="Reference 4"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-27" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-27" aria-label="Reference 27"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV6: Poison Fog. <i>Generates a toxic cloud.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN2C9_28-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2C9-28" aria-label="Reference 28"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV7: Paralysis Shot. <i>Paralyzes the target temporarily</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN2C9_28-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2C9-28" aria-label="Reference 28"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Deadly Poison Shot.<sup id="cite_ref-29" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-29" aria-label="Reference 29"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Recovery">Recovery</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D14&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Recovery_Magic">Recovery Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D15&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Healing_Magic">Healing Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D16&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Has status condition recovery spells and HP recovery spells.<sup id="cite_ref-30" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-30" aria-label="Reference 30"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>LV2: Micro Treatment. <i>Cures scratches or small injures. Can't recovery lost body parts.</i><sup id="cite_ref-31" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-31" aria-label="Reference 31"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>31<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Unknown Level: Status Shift Recovery. Removes minor ailments from targets.</li>
<li>Unknown Level: HP Recovery. Recovers a large amount of HP.</li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Miracle_Magic">Miracle Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D17&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>The higher rank of Healing Magic. Its effects are so effective that they seem to be caused by the gods. Can be awarded by <a href="/wiki/System/Titles/Ruler_Titles#Seven_Heavenly_Virtues" title="System/Titles/Ruler Titles">Ruler of Charity</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-LN5C4_32-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN5C4-32" aria-label="Reference 32"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>32<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>Unknown Level: Advanced Status Shift Recovery. Removes all ailment effects on the targets.</li>
<li>Unknown Level: Advanced HP Recovery. Recovers an incredible amount of HP, even managing to bring back those from the jaws of death.</li>
<li>Unknown Level: Unknown Name. Almost miraculous spell that is capable of reuniting lost limbs and organs, and even regenerating new ones from scratch.</li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Space">Space</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D18&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Spatial_Magic">Spatial Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D19&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>『Magic that manipulates space.』<sup id="cite_ref-33" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-33" aria-label="Reference 33"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>LV1: Coordinate Designation. <i>A movable cube made of green lines appear.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN2C9_28-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2C9-28" aria-label="Reference 28"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV2: Coordinate Fixation. <i>Fixes the designated range of coordinates. Magic aimed at the fixed coordinates has a 100% hit rate.</i><sup id="cite_ref-34" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-34" aria-label="Reference 34"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>34<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Spatial Storage. <i>Stores items in a separate dimension.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN4C10_35-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN4C10-35" aria-label="Reference 35"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>35<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Large-Scale Teleport. <i>Teleports a group.</i><sup id="cite_ref-36" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-36" aria-label="Reference 36"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>36<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Short-Range Teleport. <i>Requires only a short time to prepare.</i><sup id="cite_ref-v4c3_37-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-v4c3-37" aria-label="Reference 37"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>37<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV9: Long-Distance Teleport.<sup id="cite_ref-LN3C7_38-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN3C7-38" aria-label="Reference 38"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>38<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> <i>Teleports the user to any location they've been to before.<sup id="cite_ref-39" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-39" aria-label="Reference 39"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>39<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Requires a few minutes to prepare.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN4C3_18-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN4C3-18" aria-label="Reference 18"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Dimensional_Magic">Dimensional Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D20&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>Range Teleport. <i>Teleports everything within an area to a target destination, including unwilling targets.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN3C9_20-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN3C9-20" aria-label="Reference 20"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Earth">Earth</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D21&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p>Magic that manipulates any large solid body including metal and soil.<sup id="cite_ref-40" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-40" aria-label="Reference 40"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>40<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Earth_Magic">Earth Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D22&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li><i>Summons an earth spear from the ground.</i><sup id="cite_ref-41" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-41" aria-label="Reference 41"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>41<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li><i>Shoots a target with a ball of earth.</i><sup id="cite_ref-42" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-42" aria-label="Reference 42"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>42<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Terrain_Magic">Terrain Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D23&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>Terrain Wall. <i>Summons a wall from the ground.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN3C10_2-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN3C10-2" aria-label="Reference 2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>

<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Fire_Magic">Fire Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D26&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>LV1: Ember.<sup id="cite_ref-44" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-44" aria-label="Reference 44"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV5: Eruption. <i>Ignites a row of flames.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN2S2_3-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2S2-3" aria-label="Reference 3"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Flame_Magic">Flame Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D27&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li><i>Ranged attack that burns everything in a wide area.</i><sup id="cite_ref-45" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-45" aria-label="Reference 45"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>45<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Inferno_Magic">Inferno Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D28&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>LV1: Scorched Earth. <i>Covers the ground in flames, transforming it into a hellscape.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN3C11_7-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN3C11-7" aria-label="Reference 7"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV2: Inferno Spear. <i>An enormous spear of flames assaults the target.</i><sup id="cite_ref-46" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-46" aria-label="Reference 46"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>46<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV4: Heat Haze. <i>Compresses an incredible amount of heat into a fist-sized ball.</i><sup id="cite_ref-47" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-47" aria-label="Reference 47"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>47<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Water">Water</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D29&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Water_Magic">Water Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D30&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>LV1: Water Ball. <i>Projects a sphere of water.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN2S2_3-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN2S2-3" aria-label="Reference 3"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Water Shot. <i>Shoots a ball of water.</i><sup id="cite_ref-48" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-48" aria-label="Reference 48"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>48<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>LV2: Water Gun. Shoots a ray of water.</li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Flood_Magic">Flood Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D31&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Tide_Magic">Tide Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D32&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Wind">Wind</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D33&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Wind_Magic">Wind Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D34&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>Wind Ball. <i>Creates a fragile ball of air that can be used to breath underwater.</i><sup id="cite_ref-49" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-49" aria-label="Reference 49"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>49<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Silencing. <i>Erases all sound in the target area.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN3S4_50-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN3S4-50" aria-label="Reference 50"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>50<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li><i>Shoots a bullet of wind.</i><sup id="cite_ref-WNE6_51-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WNE6-51" aria-label="Reference 51"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>51<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Gale_Magic">Gale Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D35&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>Binding Wind.  <i>Holds target in place.</i><sup id="cite_ref-LN4S4_52-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-LN4S4-52" aria-label="Reference 52"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>52<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Sky Fall. <i>Causes a mass of air to strike the ground over a wide range.</i><sup id="cite_ref-53" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-53" aria-label="Reference 53"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>53<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Tempest_Magic">Tempest Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D36&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>LV4: Dragon Wind: <i>Generates a tornado.</i><sup id="cite_ref-WNE6_51-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WNE6-51" aria-label="Reference 51"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>51<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Ice">Ice</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D37&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p>The ice produced slowly returns to magical power, so it can't be used to satiate thirst.<sup id="cite_ref-WN176_54-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WN176-54" aria-label="Reference 54"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>54<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Ice_Magic">Ice Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D38&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li><i>Low rank magic that shoots ice.</i><sup id="cite_ref-WN176_54-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WN176-54" aria-label="Reference 54"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>54<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li><i>Shoots a spear of ice.</i><sup id="cite_ref-55" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-55" aria-label="Reference 55"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>55<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-56" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-56" aria-label="Reference 56"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>56<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>

<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Glacial_Magic">Glacial Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D40&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>Shining Mist. <i>Generates a beautiful mist that shines white. Anything that touches the mist freezes and shatters into flakes.</i><sup id="cite_ref-57" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-57" aria-label="Reference 57"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>57<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>

<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Lightning_Magic">Lightning Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D42&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>

<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Bolt_Magic">Bolt Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D43&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>Electric Engine. <i>Enhances the user's punching ability, causing the body to shine white.</i><sup id="cite_ref-58" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-58" aria-label="Reference 58"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>58<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Heavy_Magic">Heavy Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FMagic%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D45&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>

													<script data-name="featured-video-mapped-to-wiki">
	(function () {
		// we can't use mw.config here because it's too early so we need to pass this data in different way
		const videoDetails = {
			mapping: {"mediaId":"sXWp3rZ0","impressionsPerSession":4},
			impressionsPerSession: 4,
		};
		const videoBridgeCountries = ["AE","AG","AL","AM","AR","AT","AU","AZ","BA","BB","BD","BG","BH","BN","BO","BR","BS","BY","CA","CH","CL","CN","CO","CR","CU","CY","CZ","DE","DK","DO","DZ","EC","EE","EG","ES","FI","FR","GB","GE","GF","GG","GH","GP","GR","GT","GU","GY","HK","HN","HR","HU","ID","IE","IL","IM","IN","IQ","IR","IS","IT","JE","JM","JO","JP","KE","KG","KH","KR","KW","KY","KZ","LA","LB","LK","LT","LU","LV","LY","MA","MD","ME","MK","MM","MN","MO","MQ","MT","MU","MV","MX","MY","NG","NI","NL","NO","NP","NZ","OM","PA","PE","PH","PK","PL","PR","PS","PT","PY","QA","RE","RO","RS","RU","SA","SC","SE","SG","SI","SK","SV","TD","TH","TN","TR","TT","TW","UA","US","UY","UZ","VE","VI","VN","ZA","ZW"];
		const tier3VideoBridgeCountries = ["GB","BE","NL","DE","AU","MX","MY","AR","FR","MD","CA","ES","IE","IT","PE","CO","CL","NZ","PL"];
		let hasVideoOnPage = null;

		function getCookieValue(cookieName) {
			const cookieSplit = ('; ' + document.cookie).split('; ' + cookieName + '=');
			return cookieSplit.length === 2 ? cookieSplit.pop().split(';').shift() : null;
		}

		function hasMaxedOutPlayerImpressionsInWiki() {
			const impressionsSoFar = Number(getCookieValue('playerImpressionsInWiki')) || 0;
			const allowedImpressions = Number(videoDetails.impressionsPerSession);

			return impressionsSoFar >= allowedImpressions;
		}

		function getCountryCode() {
			try {
				return JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(getCookieValue('Geo'))).country.toLowerCase();
			} catch {
				return null;
			}
		}

		function isVideoBridgeAllowedForCountry() {
			const countryCode = getCountryCode();

			if (!countryCode) {
				return false;
			}

			// If it's a default mapping, then check the country whitelist from the default mapping.
			if (videoDetails?.mapping?.isDefaultMapping) {
				// If the wiki is classified as a tier3 wiki, then check the country whitelist from the tier3VideoBridge whitelist.
				const allowedCountriesForTier3Wikis = tier3VideoBridgeCountries.map(function (allowedTier3CountryCode) {
					return allowedTier3CountryCode.toLowerCase();
				})

				return allowedCountriesForTier3Wikis.indexOf(countryCode) !== -1;
			} else {
				// If not a default mapping wiki (so it's a wiki mapping), then check the country whitelist from the videoBridge whitelist
				const allowedCountries = videoBridgeCountries.map(function (allowedCountryCode) {
					return allowedCountryCode.toLowerCase();
				});

				return allowedCountries.indexOf(countryCode) !== -1;
			}
		}

		window.fandomIsVideoPossible = function () {
			if (hasVideoOnPage === null) {
				hasVideoOnPage =
					videoDetails.mapping &&
					isVideoBridgeAllowedForCountry() &&
					!hasMaxedOutPlayerImpressionsInWiki();
			}

			return hasVideoOnPage;
		};

		window.canPlayVideo = function () {
			return window.fandomIsVideoPossible()
		};

		if (!window.canPlayVideo()) {
			document.body.classList.add('no-featured-video');
		} else {
			document.body.classList.add('has-featured-video');
		}
	})();
</script>

</div>
																		<div id="mw-content-text" class="mw-body-content"><!-- Google Tag Manager (noscript) -->
<noscript><iframe src="https://www.googletagmanager.com/ns.html?id=GTM-N6XD44P" height="0" width="0" style="display:none;visibility:hidden"></iframe></noscript>
<!-- End Google Tag Manager (noscript) -->
<div class="mw-content-ltr mw-parser-output" lang="en" dir="ltr"><center><div style="margin-bottom:1em;">
<p><font color="RED"><strong>Warning: Exploring this aspect of the So I'm a Spider, So What? Wiki contains heavy spoilers.</strong></font>
</p>
</div></center>
<p>The <b>System</b> (システム, <i>Shisutemu</i>), officially the <b>W System</b> (Ｗのシステム, <i>W no shisutemu</i>), is what <a href="/wiki/Kumoko" title="Kumoko">Kumoko</a> refers to as the <a href="/wiki/System/Divine_Voice" title="System/Divine Voice">Divine Voice (temp.)</a> and the seemingly supernatural abilities it grants inhabitants from the other world. Kumoko and other reincarnations describe these mechanics to be video-game like, as the System allows living beings of the world to gain levels, <a href="/wiki/System/Skills" title="System/Skills">Skills</a>, <a href="/wiki/System/Titles" title="System/Titles">titles</a> and <a href="/wiki/System/Magic" title="System/Magic">magic</a>. 
</p><p>Unlike a video-game though, the <strong class="mw-selflink selflink">System</strong> governs real-life and culture. This creates a clear difference of power between individuals that can't always be hurdled with luck and cunning.
</p>
