<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Status">Status</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D1&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p><b>Levels</b> (レベル, <i>Reberu</i>) govern the progress of an individual or <a href="/wiki/Monsters" title="Monsters">monster</a>. When a level up is achieved, all Stats increase based on the experience of that level in addition the tier 2 and onward <a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Stat_%26_Ability_Skills" title="System/Skills/Stat &amp; Ability Skills">stat skills</a> will give a bonus based on the level of the skill. Level ups also give a Skill Proficiency Bonus to skill base on the experience of that level and skill points based on the species of the individual. In regards to <a href="/wiki/Monsters" title="Monsters">Monsters</a>, stat and ability point distribution is also given based on species, which will sometimes favor one stat over others. When a monster gains enough Levels and becomes capable of evolving, they are restricted from gaining more experience until they choose an evolution path. The amount of experience required to level up is different between species higher ranking species taking longer to level, it also takes more experience points every level.  
</p><p>It is unknown if <a href="/wiki/Humans" title="Humans">humans</a> evolve, but none have reached level 100. Levels are not as important as one might think. Although all basic stats are are increased by each level-up, certain <a href="/wiki/System/Skills" title="System/Skills">Skills</a> such as <a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Parsimonious" title="System/Skills/Parsimonious">Skanda</a> massively improve the amount raised in a given stat or ability. The experience gained in the level-up system can be roughly calculated by the amount of skills/overall experience the (killed) target has.
</p><p>Furthermore, Stats can be increased separately to Level-ups through repeated usage. For example, repeatedly emptying and refilling the HP, MP and SP bars will slowly increases their capacity. There is still a limit on how much they can be raised based on a creature's Level, however, and once that limit is reached a Level-up must occur before the stats can further increased to the new limit. When a individual or monster is a baby is when the stats grow the fastest, so intense training at this age will lead to impressively high stats that many others would be unlikely to reach, such is the case with <a href="/wiki/Sophia" title="Sophia">Sophia</a> and <a href="/wiki/Sue" title="Sue">Sue</a>.
</p><p>The maximum value for each stat with the System is 99,999. However, certain Skills like <i><a href="/wiki/Gluttony" class="mw-redirect" title="Gluttony">Gluttony</a></i> can further increase a person's stats to beyond even the System's limit, displayed in parenthesis next to the initial value. These stats must be maintained by constant use of said skills. Efficiency of Ability values can be enhanced with proper movement and knowledge.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Bars">Bars</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D2&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<figure class="thumb  show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 180px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/kumo-desu-ga-nani-ka/images/d/d4/Energy_values.png/revision/latest?cb=20190224184225" class="mw-file-description image" style="display: inline-block; position: relative;"><img alt="Anime PV1" src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/kumo-desu-ga-nani-ka/images/d/d4/Energy_values.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20190224184225" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="180" height="101" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Energy values.png" data-image-key="Energy_values.png" data-relevant="1"><div style="position: absolute; bottom: 0px; right: 0px; width:    100%; height:    100%;"><div class="gallery-icon-container view-image"><div class="icon-container view-image" aria-label="view image"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" viewBox="0 0 18 18" width="1em" height="1em"><defs><path id="IconImagesSmall__a" d="M11 15h-.586L3 7.586V7h8v8zm-8 0v-4.586L7.586 15H3zm9-10a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v10a1 1 0 0 1-1 1H2a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V6a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h10zm4-4a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v10a1 1 0 1 1-2 0V3H6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2h10z"></path></defs><use xlink:href="#IconImagesSmall__a" fill-rule="evenodd"></use></svg></div></div></div></a> 	 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Anime PV1</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<dl><dt><b>Hit Points</b> (ヒットポイント, <i>Hitto pointo</i>)</dt>
<dd><b>HP</b> is a common enough term in video games. Lose this, and death comes.</dd>
<dt><b>Magic Points</b> (マジックポイント, <i>Majikku pointo</i>)</dt>
<dd>Many creatures and individuals don't find a use for <b>MP</b> considering that it governs <a href="/wiki/System/Magic" title="System/Magic">magic</a> and skill use. The overuse of MP can cause the user to faint or lose consciousness temporarily.</dd>
<dt><b>Stamina Points</b> (スタミナポイント, <i>Sutamina pointo</i>)</dt>
<dd><b>SP</b> has two numbers within the <strong class="mw-selflink selflink">System</strong>: Yellow and Red. Yellow SP is immediately available stamina, which drains on certain ability use and recovers after a short rest. Red SP is overall energy. Running out of red SP is nearly fatal, causing a massive amount of HP and MP loss due to starvation.</dd></dl>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Abilities">Abilities</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D3&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p><b>Ability</b> represents areas of strength in an individual or <a href="/wiki/Monsters" title="Monsters">monster</a>. Unlike energy values, ability is a generalization of power. Raw numbers can make up for a deficiency in ability, and efficiency can increase their usefulness. For example, <a href="/wiki/Earth_Dragon_Araba" title="Earth Dragon Araba">Araba</a> makes use of his vast fighting experience and intelligence to enhance his Speed and Offense to the maximum of their displayed numbers. The System uses Magic energy to reinforce these values, which is why someone with high defence may not have hard skin, assuming a person has no skills and is level 1, that is their true non-system strength.  
</p>
<dl><dt><b>Average Offense Ability</b> (平均攻撃能力, <i>Heikin kōgeki nōryoku</i>)</dt>
<dd>A measure of an individual’s ability to deal <a href="/wiki/System/Attributes#Physical" title="System/Attributes">physical-attribute</a> damage. As this is an average, the power of each specific body part may be different.</dd>
<dt><b>Average Defense Ability</b> (平均防御能力, <i>Heikin bōgyo nōryoku</i>)</dt>
<dd>A measure of an individual’s ability to resist <a href="/wiki/System/Attributes#Physical" title="System/Attributes">physical-attribute</a> damage. As this is an average, the defense of each specific body part may be different.</dd>
<dt><b>Average Magic Ability</b> (平均魔法能力, <i>Heikin mahō nōryoku</i>)</dt>
<dd>A measure of the average effectiveness of an individual’s <a href="/wiki/System/Magic" title="System/Magic">magic</a>. As this is an average, the power of a specific type of magic may be different.</dd>
<dt><b>Average Resistance Ability</b> (平均抵抗能力, <i>Heikin teikō nōryoku</i>)</dt>
<dd>A measure of an individual’s ability to resist <a href="/wiki/System/Attributes#Magical" title="System/Attributes">magical-attribute</a> damage. As this is an average, the resistance may be different depending on the context and magical-attribute.</dd>
<dt><b>Average Speed Ability</b> (平均速度能力, <i>Heikin sokudo nōryoku</i>)</dt>
<dd>A measure of an individual’s average aptitude for speed. As this is an average, the quickness of each specific body part may be different. Even large monsters will dart around agilely if their speed stat is high.<sup id="cite_ref-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-1" aria-label="Reference 1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></dd></dl>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Skills">Skills</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D4&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p><i>Main article: <a href="/wiki/System/Skills" title="System/Skills">Skills</a></i><br>
<b>Skills</b> make up the vast majority of actual power within the System. Skills can be bought by having knowledge of the skill name and then spending <a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Skill_Points" title="System/Skills/Skill Points">skill points</a>, earned by gaining enough proficiency by using the skill without the assistance of the System or earnt through a <a href="/wiki/System/Titles" title="System/Titles">Title</a>. Likewise, being exposed to and surviving a certain element or attack will eventually unlock the appropriate Resistance Skill, e.g. fire damage leading to Fire Resistance skill, which can then be leveled-up through proficiency or by spending skill points. <a href="/wiki/Humans" title="Humans">Human</a> and <a href="/wiki/Demon" class="mw-redirect" title="Demon">demon</a> books have glossaries of Skills and their effects for people to learn. The unique skill <a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Other_Skills/Wisdom" title="System/Skills/Other Skills/Wisdom">Wisdom</a> grants knowledge of all possible skills the user can get, along with in-depth access to System information. Some Skills can also be learned by <a href="#Evolution">evolving</a> into an appropriate monster. The Maximum level a skill can reach is 10 upon reaching 10 certain skills can increase in tier or derive new skills. Even if a higher tier skill exists if the compatibility is to low the skill won't evolve<sup id="cite_ref-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-2" aria-label="Reference 2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. If you get a skill you already have, or get a higher tier skill the weaker skill will be integrated into the stronger skill adding the proficiency together, this most commonly occurs due to skills given through titles.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Magic">Magic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D5&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p><i>Main article: <a href="/wiki/System/Magic" title="System/Magic">Magic</a></i><br>
<b>Magic</b> is an assistance mechanism for the invocation of <a href="/wiki/Magecraft" class="mw-redirect" title="Magecraft">magecraft</a>. Each magic skill grants access to new spells as they level-up. When a magic skill reaches level 10, it will often derive a more powerful magic skill of the same <a href="/wiki/System/Attributes#Magical" title="System/Attributes">attribute</a>. <a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Evil_Eyes" title="System/Skills/Evil Eyes">Evil Eyes</a> are a rare form of <a href="/wiki/System/Magic" title="System/Magic">magic</a> that trade flashiness for reliability.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Titles">Titles</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D6&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p><i>Main article: <a href="/wiki/System/Titles" title="System/Titles">Titles</a></i><br>
<b>Titles</b> are granted to individuals when they meet certain conditions. For example, eating a member of your family will reward you the Kin Eater title. Each title awards a maximum of two Skills and most but not all also grant a passive effect.<sup id="cite_ref-V2C4_3-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-V2C4-3" aria-label="Reference 3"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> The Fearbringer title causes <a href="/wiki/System/Attributes#Heresy" title="System/Attributes">Fear</a> in those who look at holder, and grants the Skills Intimidation and <a href="/wiki/System/Attributes#Heresy" title="System/Attributes">Heresy Attack</a> on acquisition. Some titles are required for certain <a href="#Evolution">evolutions</a>, such as the evolution to <a href="/wiki/Zoa_Ele" title="Zoa Ele">Zoa Ele</a> requiring the Assassin title.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Evolution">Evolution</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D7&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p><b>Evolution</b> is an aspect of the system that is unique to <a href="/wiki/Monsters" title="Monsters">monsters</a>, although it is speculated that humans may evolve at level 100.<sup id="cite_ref-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-4" aria-label="Reference 4"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> When a <a href="/wiki/Monsters" title="Monsters">monster</a> accumulates enough experience, their <a href="#Stats">level</a> progression is halted and they are given a choice of species to evolve into. Some evolutions are locked behind certain conditions, such as the acquisition of a <a href="/wiki/System/Titles" title="System/Titles">title</a> or <a href="/wiki/System/Skills" title="System/Skills">skill</a>. Evolutions consume large amounts of SP and MP<sup id="cite_ref-V1C7_5-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-V1C7-5" aria-label="Reference 5"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> leaving weaker monster with only a single point in each. Evolutions can also give <a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Skill_Points" title="System/Skills/Skill Points">Skill Points</a> to the monster.<sup id="cite_ref-V2C6_6-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-V2C6-6" aria-label="Reference 6"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> The <a href="/wiki/Soul" title="Soul">soul</a> of the individual is gradually shaped by the species. Evolving counts as a level up raising stats, giving Bonus: Skill Proficiency, In addition some evolutions give skills. 
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Evolution_Trees">Evolution Trees</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D8&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p><a href="/wiki/Spiders" class="mw-redirect" title="Spiders">Spiders</a> &nbsp;•&nbsp; <a href="/wiki/Dragons" class="mw-redirect" title="Dragons">Dragons</a> &nbsp;•&nbsp; <a href="/wiki/Goblins" title="Goblins">Goblins</a>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Origins_of_the_System">Origins of the System</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D9&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p>The MA System is not a natural phenomenon. Due to remembering Earth, some of the reincarnations guess that this might be the case, but the truth is far more sinister.
</p><p><b>Below are spoilers from the <a href="/wiki/Past_Arc" title="Past Arc">Past Arc</a></b>
</p><p><span class="spoilerblur">It was later revealed that the the System itself is a planetary scale <a href="/wiki/Conjuring" title="Conjuring">Conjuring</a> construct designed by <a href="/wiki/D" title="D">D</a> to restore the <a href="/wiki/MA_Energy" title="MA Energy">MA Energy</a> of the planet. It is the system that governs the <a href="/wiki/System/Skills" title="System/Skills">Skills</a>, <a href="/wiki/System/Titles" title="System/Titles">Titles</a>, <a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Stats">Stats</a>, and abilities of all residents and monsters, and is what allows residents to use magic in an assisted form.</span> 
</p><p><span class="spoilerblur">It was created when a massive catastrophic occured in the past, pushing the world to the edge of collapse. In a desperate attempt to save the world, one of the gods inhabiting the planet, <a href="/wiki/G%C3%BCliedistodiez" title="Güliedistodiez">Güliedistodiez</a>, decided to sort help one of the most powerful and mysterious gods, <a href="/wiki/D" title="D">D</a>. While D obliged and had the ability to instantly restore the planet into balance, she choose to set up a roundabout way to delay the destruction and possibly fixed the planet in the long time by trapping the native inhabitants in an endless cycle of reincarnations and battles. This cycle of death will gradually heal up the planet but also forces the humans to engage in a struggle of life and death to fixes the planet. All for the amusement and entertainment for D.</span> 
</p><p><span class="spoilerblur">Without the System, magic would be impossible. The <a href="/wiki/Religion#The_Word_of_God_Religion" title="Religion">Word of God</a> religion worships the voice of the System, claiming that it is the voice of God. The System itself is governed and run by the Angel <a href="/wiki/Sariel" title="Sariel">Sariel</a>. She is the core of the System, imprisoned on the very bottom floor of the <a href="/wiki/Great_Elroe_Labyrinth" title="Great Elroe Labyrinth">Great Elroe Labyrinth</a> and the voice that people hear when <a href="/wiki/System/Skills" title="System/Skills">Skills</a> and levels are gained.</span>
</p>

													<script data-name="featured-video-mapped-to-wiki">
	(function () {
		// we can't use mw.config here because it's too early so we need to pass this data in different way
		const videoDetails = {
			mapping: {"mediaId":"sXWp3rZ0","impressionsPerSession":4},
			impressionsPerSession: 4,
		};
		const videoBridgeCountries = ["AE","AG","AL","AM","AR","AT","AU","AZ","BA","BB","BD","BG","BH","BN","BO","BR","BS","BY","CA","CH","CL","CN","CO","CR","CU","CY","CZ","DE","DK","DO","DZ","EC","EE","EG","ES","FI","FR","GB","GE","GF","GG","GH","GP","GR","GT","GU","GY","HK","HN","HR","HU","ID","IE","IL","IM","IN","IQ","IR","IS","IT","JE","JM","JO","JP","KE","KG","KH","KR","KW","KY","KZ","LA","LB","LK","LT","LU","LV","LY","MA","MD","ME","MK","MM","MN","MO","MQ","MT","MU","MV","MX","MY","NG","NI","NL","NO","NP","NZ","OM","PA","PE","PH","PK","PL","PR","PS","PT","PY","QA","RE","RO","RS","RU","SA","SC","SE","SG","SI","SK","SV","TD","TH","TN","TR","TT","TW","UA","US","UY","UZ","VE","VI","VN","ZA","ZW"];
		const tier3VideoBridgeCountries = ["GB","BE","NL","DE","AU","MX","MY","AR","FR","MD","CA","ES","IE","IT","PE","CO","CL","NZ","PL"];
		let hasVideoOnPage = null;

		function getCookieValue(cookieName) {
			const cookieSplit = ('; ' + document.cookie).split('; ' + cookieName + '=');
			return cookieSplit.length === 2 ? cookieSplit.pop().split(';').shift() : null;
		}

		function hasMaxedOutPlayerImpressionsInWiki() {
			const impressionsSoFar = Number(getCookieValue('playerImpressionsInWiki')) || 0;
			const allowedImpressions = Number(videoDetails.impressionsPerSession);

			return impressionsSoFar >= allowedImpressions;
		}

		function getCountryCode() {
			try {
				return JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(getCookieValue('Geo'))).country.toLowerCase();
			} catch {
				return null;
			}
		}

		function isVideoBridgeAllowedForCountry() {
			const countryCode = getCountryCode();

			if (!countryCode) {
				return false;
			}

			// If it's a default mapping, then check the country whitelist from the default mapping.
			if (videoDetails?.mapping?.isDefaultMapping) {
				// If the wiki is classified as a tier3 wiki, then check the country whitelist from the tier3VideoBridge whitelist.
				const allowedCountriesForTier3Wikis = tier3VideoBridgeCountries.map(function (allowedTier3CountryCode) {
					return allowedTier3CountryCode.toLowerCase();
				})

				return allowedCountriesForTier3Wikis.indexOf(countryCode) !== -1;
			} else {
				// If not a default mapping wiki (so it's a wiki mapping), then check the country whitelist from the videoBridge whitelist
				const allowedCountries = videoBridgeCountries.map(function (allowedCountryCode) {
					return allowedCountryCode.toLowerCase();
				});

				return allowedCountries.indexOf(countryCode) !== -1;
			}
		}

		window.fandomIsVideoPossible = function () {
			if (hasVideoOnPage === null) {
				hasVideoOnPage =
					videoDetails.mapping &&
					isVideoBridgeAllowedForCountry() &&
					!hasMaxedOutPlayerImpressionsInWiki();
			}

			return hasVideoOnPage;
		};

		window.canPlayVideo = function () {
			return window.fandomIsVideoPossible()
		};

		if (!window.canPlayVideo()) {
			document.body.classList.add('no-featured-video');
		} else {
			document.body.classList.add('has-featured-video');
		}
	})();
</script>

<div class="mw-content-ltr mw-parser-output" lang="en" dir="ltr"><p><b>Skills</b> are functions that exist within the <a href="/wiki/System" title="System">System</a> in the world of <i>Kumo Desu ga, Nani ka</i>. Skills are powers granted to the beings inside of the <a href="/wiki/System" title="System">System</a> based on knowledge of the skill and using <a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Skill_Points" title="System/Skills/Skill Points">skill points</a> to acquire them, or by learning through practical application of said skill.
</p><p>Skills are System-assisted <a href="/wiki/Conjuring" title="Conjuring">Conjuring</a> for convenient usage, but they are restricted to specific uses. i.e. only being able to use Space Magic spells instead of the unique skill-set <a href="/wiki/Shiraori" title="Shiraori">Shiraori</a> has with <a href="/wiki/System/Magic#Space" title="System/Magic">Space Magic</a>. Without System assistance, skills and <a href="/wiki/System/Magic" title="System/Magic">magic</a> would be almost impossible for most beings in the <a href="/wiki/World" title="World">world</a> since humans cannot perceive Magic, hence the requirement for Magic Power Perception and Operation skills required to use it.
</p><p>Please check the <a href="/wiki/Patch_Notes" title="Patch Notes">System Patch Notes</a> for skill name changes and inconsistencies.
</p>
<div id="toc" class="toc toc--initialized" role="navigation" aria-labelledby="mw-toc-heading"><input type="checkbox" role="button" id="toctogglecheckbox" class="toctogglecheckbox" style="display:none" aria-label="show"><div class="toctitle" lang="en" dir="ltr"><h2 id="mw-toc-heading"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use href="#wds-icons-bullet-list-tiny"></use></svg>Contents</h2><span class="toctogglespan"><label class="toctogglelabel" for="toctogglecheckbox" aria-label="show"></label></span></div>

													<script data-name="featured-video-mapped-to-wiki">
	(function () {
		// we can't use mw.config here because it's too early so we need to pass this data in different way
		const videoDetails = {
			mapping: {"mediaId":"sXWp3rZ0","impressionsPerSession":4},
			impressionsPerSession: 4,
		};
		const videoBridgeCountries = ["AE","AG","AL","AM","AR","AT","AU","AZ","BA","BB","BD","BG","BH","BN","BO","BR","BS","BY","CA","CH","CL","CN","CO","CR","CU","CY","CZ","DE","DK","DO","DZ","EC","EE","EG","ES","FI","FR","GB","GE","GF","GG","GH","GP","GR","GT","GU","GY","HK","HN","HR","HU","ID","IE","IL","IM","IN","IQ","IR","IS","IT","JE","JM","JO","JP","KE","KG","KH","KR","KW","KY","KZ","LA","LB","LK","LT","LU","LV","LY","MA","MD","ME","MK","MM","MN","MO","MQ","MT","MU","MV","MX","MY","NG","NI","NL","NO","NP","NZ","OM","PA","PE","PH","PK","PL","PR","PS","PT","PY","QA","RE","RO","RS","RU","SA","SC","SE","SG","SI","SK","SV","TD","TH","TN","TR","TT","TW","UA","US","UY","UZ","VE","VI","VN","ZA","ZW"];
		const tier3VideoBridgeCountries = ["GB","BE","NL","DE","AU","MX","MY","AR","FR","MD","CA","ES","IE","IT","PE","CO","CL","NZ","PL"];
		let hasVideoOnPage = null;

		function getCookieValue(cookieName) {
			const cookieSplit = ('; ' + document.cookie).split('; ' + cookieName + '=');
			return cookieSplit.length === 2 ? cookieSplit.pop().split(';').shift() : null;
		}

		function hasMaxedOutPlayerImpressionsInWiki() {
			const impressionsSoFar = Number(getCookieValue('playerImpressionsInWiki')) || 0;
			const allowedImpressions = Number(videoDetails.impressionsPerSession);

			return impressionsSoFar >= allowedImpressions;
		}

		function getCountryCode() {
			try {
				return JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(getCookieValue('Geo'))).country.toLowerCase();
			} catch {
				return null;
			}
		}

		function isVideoBridgeAllowedForCountry() {
			const countryCode = getCountryCode();

			if (!countryCode) {
				return false;
			}

			// If it's a default mapping, then check the country whitelist from the default mapping.
			if (videoDetails?.mapping?.isDefaultMapping) {
				// If the wiki is classified as a tier3 wiki, then check the country whitelist from the tier3VideoBridge whitelist.
				const allowedCountriesForTier3Wikis = tier3VideoBridgeCountries.map(function (allowedTier3CountryCode) {
					return allowedTier3CountryCode.toLowerCase();
				})

				return allowedCountriesForTier3Wikis.indexOf(countryCode) !== -1;
			} else {
				// If not a default mapping wiki (so it's a wiki mapping), then check the country whitelist from the videoBridge whitelist
				const allowedCountries = videoBridgeCountries.map(function (allowedCountryCode) {
					return allowedCountryCode.toLowerCase();
				});

				return allowedCountries.indexOf(countryCode) !== -1;
			}
		}

		window.fandomIsVideoPossible = function () {
			if (hasVideoOnPage === null) {
				hasVideoOnPage =
					videoDetails.mapping &&
					isVideoBridgeAllowedForCountry() &&
					!hasMaxedOutPlayerImpressionsInWiki();
			}

			return hasVideoOnPage;
		};

		window.canPlayVideo = function () {
			return window.fandomIsVideoPossible()
		};

		if (!window.canPlayVideo()) {
			document.body.classList.add('no-featured-video');
		} else {
			document.body.classList.add('has-featured-video');
		}
	})();
</script>

<noscript><iframe src="https://www.googletagmanager.com/ns.html?id=GTM-N6XD44P" height="0" width="0" style="display:none;visibility:hidden"></iframe></noscript>

<div class="mw-content-ltr mw-parser-output" lang="en" dir="ltr"><p><br>
<b>Attributes</b> classify damage types and <a href="/wiki/Status_Conditions" class="mw-redirect" title="Status Conditions">Status Conditions</a>. The defense ability value protects against physical attribute damage, whereas the resistance ability value protects against magical attribute damage. Each individual has specific defenses and resistances, which are averaged to give the value seen in status appraisal results. <a href="/wiki/System/Skills#Resistance_Skills" title="System/Skills">Resistance skills</a> raise these individual defenses and resistances; nullification <a href="/wiki/Skills" class="mw-redirect" title="Skills">skills</a> set their attribute's resistance value to 99,999. Damage within the system is arbitrary—water and fire generally do equal damage.<sup id="cite_ref-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-1" aria-label="Reference 1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Each individual has their own affinity for types of <a href="/wiki/System/Magic" title="System/Magic">magic</a>. This affinity can be changed by acquiring <a href="/wiki/Titles" class="mw-redirect" title="Titles">titles</a> such as <a href="/wiki/System/Titles#Normal_Titles" title="System/Titles">Saviour</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-WN184_2-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WN184-2" aria-label="Reference 2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div id="toc" class="toc toc--initialized" role="navigation" aria-labelledby="mw-toc-heading"><input type="checkbox" role="button" id="toctogglecheckbox" class="toctogglecheckbox" style="display:none" aria-label="show"><div class="toctitle" lang="en" dir="ltr"><h2 id="mw-toc-heading"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use href="#wds-icons-bullet-list-tiny"></use></svg>Contents</h2><span class="toctogglespan"><label class="toctogglelabel" for="toctogglecheckbox" aria-label="show"></label></span></div>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Physical">Physical</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FAttributes%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D1&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Magical">Magical</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FAttributes%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D2&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<dl><dd><b>Main Article:</b> <a href="/wiki/Magic" class="mw-redirect" title="Magic">Magic</a></dd></dl>
<table class="article-table">
<tbody><tr>
<th>Attribute
</th>
<th>Skills
</th>
<th>Description
</th></tr>
<tr>
<td>Heresy
</td>
<td>Heretic Magic<br>Heresy Resistance
<p>Heresy Evil Eyes
</p>
</td>
<td>The attribute that affects ones soul
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Fire
</td>
<td>Fire Magic<br>Fire Enhancement<br>Fire Resistance<br>Flame Attack<br>Fire Wyrm<br>Heat Perception
</td>
<td>Only low-level fire magic will consume oxygen or produce carbon dioxide.<sup id="cite_ref-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-3" aria-label="Reference 3"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Water
</td>
<td>Water Magic<br>Flood Magic<br><i>Azure Seas Magic</i><br>Water Resistance
</td>
<td>Is considered one of if not the most safe attribute, making it common to be used
<p>when first learning magic
</p>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Ice
</td>
<td><i>Ice Magic</i><br><i>Freeze Magic</i><br><i>Frozen Prison Magic</i><br>Ice Resistance
</td>
<td>Ice affinity corelates with water affinity
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Wind
</td>
<td>Wind Magic<br>Gale Magic<br>Tempest Magic<br>Wind Resistance
</td>
<td>As it manipulates air, blasted air can be used against things that have an
<p>anti-technique barrier  
</p>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Earth
</td>
<td>Earth Magic<br>Terrain Magic<br>Seismic Magic<br>Earth Resistance<br>Terrain Attack<br>Earth Wyrm
</td>
<td>Earth magic is one of the best ways of bypassing the dragon scales skills as it
<p>is mostly physical
</p>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Lightning
</td>
<td>Lightning Magic<br>Bolt Magic<br>Lightning Resistance
</td>
<td>Lightning affinity correlates with Light affinity.<sup id="cite_ref-WN184_2-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WN184-2" aria-label="Reference 2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Light
</td>
<td>Light Magic<br>Holy Light Magic<br>Light Enhancement<br>Holy Light Resistance<br>Light Attack<br>Light Wyrm
</td>
<td>Titles that are given based on the receiving of purification points, increase
<p>light magic affinity 
</p>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Dark
</td>
<td>Shadow Magic<br>Dark Magic<br>Black Magic<br>Abyss Magic<br>Dark Resistance
</td>
<td>Cancels out Light Magic.<sup id="cite_ref-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-4" aria-label="Reference 4"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Is unaffected by water.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Poison
</td>
<td>Poison Magic<br>Healing Magic<br><i>Miracle Magic</i><br>Poison Resistance<br>Status Condition Resistance<br>Poison Fang<br>Poison Attack<br>Poison Enhancement<br>Poison Synthesis
</td>
<td>Poison magic can also cause paralysis
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Space
</td>
<td>Spatial Magic<br>Dimensional Magic<br>Spatial Perception<br>Spatial Maneuvering
</td>
<td>Most complicated and rarest attribute, can only be targeted at relative areas
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Heavy
</td>
<td>Heavy Magic<br><a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Evil_Eyes" title="System/Skills/Evil Eyes">Heavy Evil Eye</a><br>Heavy Resistance<br>Heavy Armor<br>Heavy Attack
</td>
<td>Harder to control in water(<i><b>Unconfirmed</b></i>).<sup id="cite_ref-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-5" aria-label="Reference 5"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Advanced form can push and pull
<p>in not just the direction of gravity
</p>
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Curse
</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Evil_Eyes" title="System/Skills/Evil Eyes">Cursed Evil Eye</a><br><a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Evil_Eyes" title="System/Skills/Evil Eyes">Jinx Evil Eye</a><br><i>Jinx Magic</i>
</td>
<td>Weakens all stat values and causes damage to HP, MP, and SP.<sup id="cite_ref-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-6" aria-label="Reference 6"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</td></tr></tbody></table>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Heresy">Heresy</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FAttributes%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D3&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p>Attribute that affects the soul. Resisted by <b>Heresy Resistance</b>
</p>
<table class="article-table">
<tbody><tr>
<th>Attribute
</th>
<th>Skills
</th>
<th>Description
</th></tr>
<tr>
<td>Rend Soul (破魂)
</td>
<td>Heretic Attack
</td>
<td>Damages the soul
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><b>Hypnotized effect</b> (催眠)
</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Evil_Eyes" title="System/Skills/Evil Eyes">Hypnotizing Evil Eye</a>
</td>
<td>Forces the target to obey the user
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Brainwashed effect (洗脳)
</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Lust" class="mw-redirect" title="Lust">Lust</a>
</td>
<td>It becomes possible to gain control over any individual, as long
<p>as the brainwashing is reapplied<sup id="cite_ref-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-7" aria-label="Reference 7"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, but only by combining it with
other effects like Charm and Hypnotized, can one be made to do
things they are completely unwilling to do<sup id="cite_ref-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-8" aria-label="Reference 8"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Charmed effect (魅了)
</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Evil_Eyes" title="System/Skills/Evil Eyes">Charming Evil Eye</a>
</td>
<td>Makes the target charmed by the user
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Madness effect (狂気)
</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Evil_Eyes" title="System/Skills/Evil Eyes">Maddening Evil Eye</a><br>Rage
</td>
<td>Makes the affected become overcome with anger
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Discomfort effect (不快)
</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Evil_Eyes" title="System/Skills/Evil Eyes">Discomforting Evil Eye</a>
</td>
<td>target feels an unsettling feeling
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Phantom Pain effect (幻痛)
</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Evil_Eyes" title="System/Skills/Evil Eyes">Phantom Pain Evil Eye</a><br>Pain Resistance<br>Pain Mitigation<br>Faint Resistance
</td>
<td>Causes pain that ignores the Pain Mitigation skill
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Fear effect (恐怖)
</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Evil_Eyes" title="System/Skills/Evil Eyes">Fearful Evil Eye</a><br>Fear Resistance<br>Intimidation
</td>
<td>Makes those affected instictively fear the source
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Stupefaction effect (自失)
</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Evil_Eyes" title="System/Skills/Evil Eyes">Stupefaction Evil Eye</a>
</td>
<td>Reduces targets intelligence
</td></tr></tbody></table>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Other">Other</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fkumodesu.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FSystem%2FAttributes%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D4&amp;uselang=en&amp;metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section" data-auth-modal="signin" data-testid="log-in-edit-section" aria-label="Sign in to edit"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>

<tbody><tr>
<th>Attribute
</th>
<th>Skills

<th>Description
</th></tr>
<tr>
<td>Paralysis
</td>
<td>Paralyzing Evil Eye<br>Paralysis Resistance<br>Status Condition Resistance<br>Paralysis Attack
</td>
<td>Reduces movement until being unable to move,
<p>its advanced form Inert causing a time freeze affect
</p>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Petrification
</td>
<td>Petrifying Evil Eye<br>Petrification Resistance<br>Status Condition Resistance
</td>
<td>Causes body to turn to stone
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Sleep
</td>
<td>Exhaustion Nullification<br>Status Condition Resistance
</td>
<td>Is asleep or forced to be asleep, its advanced
<p>form being Coma
</p>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td>Acid
</td>
<td>Acid Enhancement<br>Acid Resistance<br>Acid Attack
</td>
<td>Can ignore external defense and destroy Items
</td></tr>
<tr>

</td>
<td>Annihilating Evil Eye<br>Rot Resistance<br>Rot Attack
</td>
<td>Attribute that governs death, normally only works
<p>on living things
</p>
</td></tr></tbody></table>

													<script data-name="featured-video-mapped-to-wiki">
	(function () {
		// we can't use mw.config here because it's too early so we need to pass this data in different way
		const videoDetails = {
			mapping: {"mediaId":"sXWp3rZ0","impressionsPerSession":4},
			impressionsPerSession: 4,
		};
		const videoBridgeCountries = ["AE","AG","AL","AM","AR","AT","AU","AZ","BA","BB","BD","BG","BH","BN","BO","BR","BS","BY","CA","CH","CL","CN","CO","CR","CU","CY","CZ","DE","DK","DO","DZ","EC","EE","EG","ES","FI","FR","GB","GE","GF","GG","GH","GP","GR","GT","GU","GY","HK","HN","HR","HU","ID","IE","IL","IM","IN","IQ","IR","IS","IT","JE","JM","JO","JP","KE","KG","KH","KR","KW","KY","KZ","LA","LB","LK","LT","LU","LV","LY","MA","MD","ME","MK","MM","MN","MO","MQ","MT","MU","MV","MX","MY","NG","NI","NL","NO","NP","NZ","OM","PA","PE","PH","PK","PL","PR","PS","PT","PY","QA","RE","RO","RS","RU","SA","SC","SE","SG","SI","SK","SV","TD","TH","TN","TR","TT","TW","UA","US","UY","UZ","VE","VI","VN","ZA","ZW"];
		const tier3VideoBridgeCountries = ["GB","BE","NL","DE","AU","MX","MY","AR","FR","MD","CA","ES","IE","IT","PE","CO","CL","NZ","PL"];
		let hasVideoOnPage = null;

		function getCookieValue(cookieName) {
			const cookieSplit = ('; ' + document.cookie).split('; ' + cookieName + '=');
			return cookieSplit.length === 2 ? cookieSplit.pop().split(';').shift() : null;
		}

		function hasMaxedOutPlayerImpressionsInWiki() {
			const impressionsSoFar = Number(getCookieValue('playerImpressionsInWiki')) || 0;
			const allowedImpressions = Number(videoDetails.impressionsPerSession);

			return impressionsSoFar >= allowedImpressions;
		}

		function getCountryCode() {
			try {
				return JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(getCookieValue('Geo'))).country.toLowerCase();
			} catch {
				return null;
			}
		}

		function isVideoBridgeAllowedForCountry() {
			const countryCode = getCountryCode();

			if (!countryCode) {
				return false;
			}

			// If it's a default mapping, then check the country whitelist from the default mapping.
			if (videoDetails?.mapping?.isDefaultMapping) {
				// If the wiki is classified as a tier3 wiki, then check the country whitelist from the tier3VideoBridge whitelist.
				const allowedCountriesForTier3Wikis = tier3VideoBridgeCountries.map(function (allowedTier3CountryCode) {
					return allowedTier3CountryCode.toLowerCase();
				})

				return allowedCountriesForTier3Wikis.indexOf(countryCode) !== -1;
			} else {
				// If not a default mapping wiki (so it's a wiki mapping), then check the country whitelist from the videoBridge whitelist
				const allowedCountries = videoBridgeCountries.map(function (allowedCountryCode) {
					return allowedCountryCode.toLowerCase();
				});

				return allowedCountries.indexOf(countryCode) !== -1;
			}
		}

		window.fandomIsVideoPossible = function () {
			if (hasVideoOnPage === null) {
				hasVideoOnPage =
					videoDetails.mapping &&
					isVideoBridgeAllowedForCountry() &&
					!hasMaxedOutPlayerImpressionsInWiki();
			}

			return hasVideoOnPage;
		};

		window.canPlayVideo = function () {
			return window.fandomIsVideoPossible()
		};

		if (!window.canPlayVideo()) {
			document.body.classList.add('no-featured-video');
		} else {
			document.body.classList.add('has-featured-video');
		}
	})();
</script>

<div class="mw-content-ltr mw-parser-output" lang="en" dir="ltr"><p><br>
<b>Titles</b> (称号, <i>Shōgō</i>) are the reinforced code acquired by meeting specific conditions. When obtaining one, two <a href="/wiki/System/Skills" title="System/Skills">Skills</a> can be acquired. In contrast to <a href="/wiki/System/Skills" title="System/Skills">Skills</a>, titles awarded by the <a href="/wiki/System" title="System">system</a> without a fair exchange for <a href="/wiki/System/Skills/Skill_Points" title="System/Skills/Skill Points">skill points</a> or proficiency.<sup id="cite_ref-Web_novel_240:_The_Hero_title_1-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Web_novel_240:_The_Hero_title-1" aria-label="Reference 1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
